- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-agents-md-001]] — The required environment variables for the project are OPENROUTER_API_KEY, ALPACA_API_KEY, and ALPAC
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-agents-md-002]] — The configuration source of truth is data/config/settings.yaml, which must always be loaded via load

- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-agents-md-005]] — src/services/ must not import from agents or orchestration, a constraint enforced by tests/unit/arch
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-agents-md-006]] — The project's test coverage target is approximately 58–59% for the overall project and 70% or higher

- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-000]] — Alpaca Agents is a multi-agent AI trading system for Alpaca Markets built on LangGraph.
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-001]] — The system's current status is Production-Ready for Paper Trading.
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-002]] — The agent pipeline flows from Research → Market Data → Technical and Sentiment into Risk → Portfolio
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-003]] — All agents mutate a shared TradingState object defined in src/models/state.py rather than reassignin
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-004]] — The system defines four agent patterns: ReAct (dynamic tool selection), Direct (fixed sequence), Hyb
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-005]] — The deliberation system triggers a Bullish ↔ Bearish → Synthesizer debate at 60–95% confidence.
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-006]] — Model routing maps simple tasks to haiku, moderate tasks to sonnet, and complex tasks to opus.
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-007]] — The token budget is configured at 50000 max per workflow with a warning threshold at 40000.
- [[claim-alpaca-agents-alpaca-agents-claude-md-008]] — Expert Advisors consist of 14 investor personas used for deliberation consultation.

- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-003]] — The import system supports three external platforms: AniList, MyAnimeList, and Kitsu.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-004]] — The unified ingestion pipeline orchestrates four phases (populate, extract, commit, validate) in a s
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-005]] — The populate phase is the only phase that calls upstream APIs, performing seed source and cross-plat
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-006]] — The commit phase atomically writes media_titles, media_source_mapping, and media_relations in one tr
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-007]] — The pipeline uses exit codes 0=success, 2=populate, 3=extract, 4=commit, 5=validate, and 64=usage er
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-008]] — Cache freshness is tier-based by media status: finished/cancelled media is cached for 90 days, curre
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-009]] — The backend uses FastAPI version 0.104.1 with SQLAlchemy 2.0 in async mode.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-010]] — The ML/AI stack includes PyTorch, LangChain, LangGraph, and sentence-transformers.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-readme-md-011]] — The frontend uses Next.js 14 with App Router, TypeScript, shadcn/ui, Tailwind CSS, and React Query.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-000]] — NomikaiList is continuously deployed and only the current production release (latest main) receives 
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-001]] — Security vulnerabilities must not be reported as public issues on the repository.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-002]] — Vulnerabilities are to be reported privately through GitHub's private vulnerability reporting featur
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-003]] — The project commits to acknowledging a vulnerability report within 3 business days.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-004]] — The project commits to completing triage and initial assessment, including a severity rating and rep
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-005]] — The project aims to remediate high and critical severity issues within 30 days.
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-006]] — The security scope covers the NomikaiList backend API, frontend, and ingestion pipeline in the repos
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-007]] — Vulnerabilities in third-party platforms (Supabase, AniList, MyAnimeList, Kitsu) are out of scope an
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-008]] — Data-access controls in NomikaiList are enforced at the database layer via Supabase Row Level Securi
- [[claim-nomokailist-nomokailist-security-md-009]] — Privacy and GDPR concerns involving personal data can be directed to privacy@nomikailist.com.
